    The softness observed in the Brazilian and Guyanese markets
followed a sharp decline in FOB cash differentials in West Africa,
a key competitive long-haul market.

    While Nigerian crudes benefited from domestic demand in
the form of the Dangote refinery, eastern-facing crudes suffered
from lackluster Chinese buying, pooling supply in the West of
Suez region.

    Platts assessed Congolese grade Djeno as low as a $34.70/
barrel discount to Dated Brent on a FOB basis in October, its all-
time low.

    "It really is the worst I have ever seen in any of the three
markets... just feels like a race to the bottom. Add to that new
Guyana production coming online in Dec and it is very difficult to
get constructive even from these levels," said the same Europe-
based trader.

    With Guyana's newest Uaru Gold crude entering the market
in December, concerns about oversupply continued to weigh on
market sentiment.

    In August 2026, the US Departments of State and Defense
signed an agreement with NABEP, giving the US a 35% interest
in the company and preferential access to purchase 20%
production at cost.

    NABEP, which retains operational control, has the rights to
commercialize more than 65 billion barrels of proven oil reserves
in Venezuela and plans to invest nearly $100 billion to increase
production capacity and improve oil and gas infrastructure, the
company says. The company has increased its crude oil output
to more than 200,000 barrels per day, compared with 18,000 b/d
two years ago with an investment of some $1 billion, NABEP said
Oct. 13.

    ExxonMobil continues to maximize diesel production as it
continues with upgrades to its US Gulf Coast refineries begun
before the current tightness in the global diesel market, company
executives said on Oct. 30.

    With expectations of tight refined product markets well into
at least 2027, ExxonMobil's signature countercyclical investment
strategies have paid off, with projects like "Growing the Gulf"
already underway.

    "We've been really on this journey of transforming the
competitiveness of our refining circuit by investing selectively and
refineries, which have scale, which have an integrated portfolio of
high-value products," said CEO Darren Woods on the third quarter
results call.

    "And the organization has now been very focused on how can
we continue to high grade our products late and improve the
yields across those refineries," he added.

Hectoliter Pure Alcohol (HPA)                                                                    BFF PGEEO00 lhc 2 QR EUR HLT Platts FOLicht Ethanol REN 96 Grade DAP
· Type: Volume Unit                                                                                                                                              West Europe Eur/HLT Contract Qtly
· Definition: A unit of volume that measures the volume of pure
(100%) alcohol. One HLTPA equals 1 hectoliter (100 liters) of pure                               BFF PGEEN03 lhc 3 MA EUR HLT Platts FOLicht Ethanol REN 96 Grade DAP
alcohol. Unlike a standard hectoliter (hL), which measures the                                                                                                   West Europe Eur/HLT MAvg
total volume of any liquid, HLTPA refers only to the pure alcohol
content, regardless of the concentration in the original beverage.                               BFF PGEEN04 lhc 2 WA EUR HLT Platts FOLicht Ethanol REN 96 Grade DAP
